# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# LDO-LOW-VOLTAGE-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.0 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 5e-10 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

Line and light/heavy-load PSRR are unscored reference measurements. Final
verification is 108 public, 18 independent and three characterization cases.
Each nominal group has six cases. No noise score is imposed on this variant.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

import json,sys
for fn in sys.argv[1:]:
 r=json.load(open(fn))
 print(fn,'ok',r.get('ok'),'revision',r.get('revision'),'pvt',r.get('pvt'),'cl',r.get('cl'))
 if not r.get('cases'): print(r);continue
 for c in r['cases']:
  m=c.get('measurements',{})
  if 'envelope' in m:
   e=m['envelope'];print(c['case_id'],c['status'],'v',round(m.get('vout_v',0),6),'Iq(uA)',round(m.get('overhead_a',0)*1e6,3),'minmax',round(e['min_v'],6),round(e['max_v'],6),'recover(us)',[round(x['recovery_s']*1e6,4) if x['recovery_s'] is not None else None for x in m['recovery_windows']])
  else: print(c['case_id'],c['status'],'v',round(m.get('vout_v',0),6),'Iq(uA)',round(m.get('overhead_a',0)*1e6,3),{k:v for k,v in m.items() if 'psrr' in k})
  if c.get('solver_errors'):print(c['solver_errors'])
 for m in r.get('summary',{}).get('spec_table',[]):
  if m['verdict']"'!='"'PASS':print('  ',m['case_id'],m['metric'],m['verdict'],m['measured'])
 print('remaining_seconds',r.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds'))
PY"

/bin/bash -lc "cat > audit_matrix.py <<'PY'
import json,sys,hashlib
from pathlib import Path
expected={(p,c) for p in ['N','SLC','SLH','SHC','SHH','FLC','FLH','FHC','FHH'] for c in [2e-10,5e-10]}
seen={}; revisions=set(); failures=[]; dc=[]; transient=[]; overhead=[]; recovery=[]
source_hash=hashlib.sha256(Path('dut.spice').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
for fn in sys.argv[1:]:
 r=json.loads(Path(fn).read_text())
 revisions.add(r['revision'])
 key=(r['pvt'],r['cl'])
 if key in seen: raise ValueError(f'duplicate group {key}')
 seen[key]=fn
 if r['analysis']"'!='"'nominal' or len(r['cases'])"'!=6:failures.append((fn,'"'incomplete'))
 params=r.get('summary',{}).get('params',{})
 if params.get('source_sha256')"'!=source_hash:failures.append((fn,'"'source differs from dut.spice'))
 for case in r['cases']:
  if case['status']"'!='"'OK':failures.append((fn,case['case_id'],case['status']))
 for m in r['summary']['spec_table']:
  if m['verdict']"'!='"'PASS':failures.append((fn,m['case_id'],m['metric'],m['verdict'],m['measured']))
  if m['metric']=='dc_error_v': dc.append(m['measured'])
  if m['metric']=='envelope_error_v':transient.append(m['measured'])
  if m['metric']=='overhead_a':overhead.append((m['case_id'],m['measured']))
  if m['metric'].startswith('recovery_') and m['measured'] is not None:recovery.append(m['measured'])
missing=expected-set(seen)
print(json.dumps(dict(groups=len(seen),cases=sum(len(json.loads(Path(x).read_text())['cases']) for x in sys.argv[1:]),revisions=list(revisions),source_hash=source_hash,missing=sorted(missing),failures=failures,max_dc_error_v=max(dc),max_transient_error_v=max(transient),max_recovery_s=max(recovery),max_light_overhead_a=max(v for k,v in overhead if k=='dc_low'),max_overhead_a=max(v for k,v in overhead)),indent=2))
if missing or failures or len(revisions)"'!=1:sys.exit(1)
PY'

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import json
from pathlib import Path
r=json.loads(Path('PUBLIC_AUDIT.json').read_text())
v=json.loads(Path('VALIDATION.json').read_text())
p=v['normalized_params']['resources']
text=f'''The submitted continuous-time LDO uses only the approved SKY130 1.8 V NFET/PFET wrappers and positive resistors and capacitors. Its four ports are VIN, VOUT, VSS, and VREF.

A 400 um total-width PMOS pass transistor supplies the load at low supply headroom. The reference divider produces 0.2 V from the ideal 0.5 V reference; a 40 kohm / 10 kohm output divider matches that level at 1.0 V. This low amplifier common-mode voltage preserves input-pair and bias headroom. A PMOS differential pair feeds a current-mirror amplifier with threefold output current gain and a small PMOS mirror ratio adjustment. An NMOS-derived resistor bias and a wide PMOS tail mirror limit variation of amplifier consumption across process and temperature.

A 10 pF capacitor across the upper feedback resistor provides fast feedback. The 1.6 pF capacitor and 10 kohm resistor between the output and pass gate damp the loop. A 15 pF reference bypass limits transient movement of the divided reference.

The exact source SHA-256 is {r['source_hash']}. All 108 public native cases, comprising 18 PVT/capacitance groups and six cases per group, passed on revision {r['revisions'][0]}. The audit confirms one revision, complete coverage, and no failed checks.

| Metric | Worst measured | Limit |
|---|---:|---:|
| DC voltage error | {r['max_dc_error_v']*1e3:.3f} mV | 30 mV |
| Load-transient voltage error | {r['max_transient_error_v']*1e3:.3f} mV | 80 mV |
| Recovery into absolute 3% band | {r['max_recovery_s']*1e6:.3f} us | 5 us |
| Light-load overhead | {r['max_light_overhead_a']*1e6:.3f} uA | 100 uA |
| Added explicit capacitance | {p['total_capacitance_f']*1e12:.1f} pF | 30 pF |
| MOS width-length proxy | {p['mos_wl_um2']:.1f} um2 | 10000 um2 |

Public verification uses the specified standard grid. Independent and characterization evaluation is performed by the client after the circuit is frozen at submission.
'''
Path('DESIGN_REPORT.md').write_text(text)
rationale='PMOS pass LDO with a PMOS input pair sensing matched 0.2 V reference and feedback levels for low-supply headroom. An NMOS-derived bias, wide PMOS tail mirror, and threefold current-mirror output gain provide fast gate drive within the light-load overhead limit. A 10 pF feedback capacitor, 1.6 pF / 10 kohm output-to-gate compensation branch, and 15 pF reference bypass give stable load recovery. The exact fixed netlist passed all 108 public cases: maximum DC error 23.674 mV, transient error 63.751 mV, recovery 0.242 us, and light-load overhead 90.756 uA. Added capacitance is 26.6 pF and MOS width-length proxy is 1134.5 um2.'
Path('REQUEST.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'submit','rationale':rationale},indent=2)+'\\n')
